# Skills · F · part 34

759 entries, figma-designer to figma-sync. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **figma-designer**: Design inside Figma through the Figma MCP with real variables, text styles, auto layout, components and token rules. ([Adnova-Group/muster](https://github.com/Adnova-Group/muster/tree/HEAD/plugin/builtins/elaya-figma-designer) · ★2)
- **figma-designer**: Analyzes Figma designs and generates implementation-ready PRDs with detailed visual specifications. Use when user provides Figma link or uploads design screenshots. Requires Figma MCP server connection. ([Reihantt6/sns-myagent](https://github.com/Reihantt6/sns-myagent/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/figma-designer) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **figma-designer**: Core design knowledge skill for any Figma work. Load this skill before designing or building anything in Figma — components, screens, design systems, layouts, or anything visual. This is not about Figma API mechanics (those are covered elsewhere). This is the internalized knowledge of a senior product designer: what makes a design accessible, consistent, usable, and polished. Triggers on any request to design, create, or build UI — whether that's a single component, a screen, or a full design system. ([JonnySLT/ai-ux-toolkit](https://github.com/JonnySLT/ai-ux-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/figma-design-system/skills/figma-designer) · ★1)
- **figma-designer**: 按照本设计师的方法在 Figma 中设计：使用真实变量、文本样式、自动布局和组件，并遵循固定视觉体系，包括 Tailwind 字号与圆角比例、间距设计变量、双层颜色设计变量、按钮和状态规则。只要通过 Figma MCP 在 Figma 文件中创建、编辑或评审屏幕、落地页、组件、模型稿、设计系统、设计变量或任何界面，就使用本技能。即使用户没有点名技能，只说‘在 Figma 中设计一个页面’‘做一个按钮组件’‘把它做成模型稿’，或分享 figma.com 链接，也要使用。在编写任何 use_figma 代码之前加载。 ([NAMEWTA/skills-hub](https://github.com/NAMEWTA/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/materials/ai-design-skills/zh/figma-designer) · ★1)

- **figma-devtools-qa**: 透過 Figma MCP 與 Chrome DevTools MCP 執行兩階段 design-to-code QA。 Phase 1（Meta 驗證）：從 Figma node 取得設計規格，注入 extract_styles.js 提取瀏覽器 computed CSS， 依 preset（container/text/surface/image）逐屬性比對間距、字體、顏色、圓角、陰影等，輸出 PASS/FAIL 報告。 Phase 2（視覺驗證）：擷取 Figma 截圖與瀏覽器截圖並排比較，標注肉眼可見差異。 FAIL 項目直接修正後重新驗證。 觸發時機：使用者說「對照 Figma 驗證」、「QA 實作結果」、「確認間距是否一致」、 「這樣有符合設計嗎」、「和 Figma 比較」，或完成 design-to-code 任務後提及 QA。 ([alexlautw9527/dot-agents](https://github.com/alexlautw9527/dot-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/figma-devtools-qa) · ★1)

Triggers on "build X in Figma", the first Figma write request of a session, or whenever figma-ds-build is about to run without a preflight.
